    Mission Statement                                                   宗旨
    Perennial International Limited is dedicated to maintain the        恒都集團有限公司致力提供優質產品、嚴格監控生
    high quality of its products, manufacturing processes and service   產過程、以及奉行以客為本的精神，務求達到盡善
    to customers and to forge a worldwide reputation as a business      盡美，讓集團聲名遠播，享譽全球。
    demanding excellence in all of its operations.

    Corporate Profile                                                   集團簡介
    The Perennial Group, founded in 1989, manufactures and trades       恒都集團於一九八九年成立，專門製造及營銷優質
    quality wire harnesses, power cords, power cord sets, cables and    的線束、電源線、電源線組合、導線及塑料樹脂。
    solid wire and plastic resins. The Group’s primary markets are      本集團主要的外銷市場是美洲、歐洲、澳洲、中國
    America, Europe, Australia, Mainland China, Japan and Southeast     大陸、日本及東南亞，客戶對象為著名的跨國電器
    Asia where it sells to prominent multi-national producers of        及電子產品生產商。
    electrical and electronic products.

    Headquartered in Hong Kong, as at 31st December 2023,               本集團總部設於香港，於二零二三年十二月三十一
    the Group employed 730 full time staff worldwide involved in        日，在全球僱用730名全職員工，負責管理、銷售
    management, sales and marketing, shipping, procurement,             及市場推廣、船務、採購、財務及會計、工程及生
    financial and accounting, engineering, production and               產等工作。本集團視員工為最寶貴的資產，而優
    manufacturing. The Group considers its employees to be its most     質、審慎及誠信則為最重要的價值。
    important asset while its key values are quality, prudence and
    integrity.

Future Prospect                                                       未來展望
Perseverance was once again demonstrated as the Group adeptly         本集團於二零二三年靈活地度過了業務環境的周期
navigated the cyclical nature of business environment, challenges     變遷、各種挑戰及逆境，我們的努力再一次得到回
and adversity in 2023. Throughout the year, the persistent global     報。事實證明，全年持續對全球宏觀經濟的挑戰相
macroeconomic challenges have proven tenacious, with the              當嚴峻，加上利率攀升、通脹壓力及不斷升級的地
combination of mounting interest rates, inflationary pressure and     緣政治緊張局勢正影響著全球的經濟格局，令營商
the escalating geopolitical tensions shaping the global economic      環境十分波動及充滿挑戰。除此等挑戰外，對於在
landscape, rendering the business environment both volatile           中國設有生產設施的公司而言，主要經濟體貿易政
and challenging. In addition to these challenges, trade policies      策的不確定性、貿易保護主義及各方關稅糾紛均令
uncertainties, trade protectionism and tariff disputes between        國際營商環境進一步複雜化。在此背景下，加上本
major economies have further complicated the international            集團於中國和香港的物業價值預期會進一步下跌，
business environment for companies with production facilities         董事遂採取更為保守的措施以減少負債及暫停派付
in the PRC. Against this backdrop, with the view of further           股息以節省現金以及進行自身戰略定位以維持競爭
deterioration of the Group’s properties values in the PRC and         力及靈活性。
Hong Kong, the Directors adopted a more conservative approach
in reducing debt and held back on dividend payment to conserve
cash and positioning itself strategically to remain competitive and
relevant.

As the Group approaches the year 2024, there is an expectation        隨著本集團邁進二零二四年，預期此等宏觀經濟因
that the delicate nature of these macroeconomic factors will          素仍會持續，包括美元難以預期的波動和十一月總
persist, the unpredictable movement of the US Dollar and              統大選結果的不確定，表明了美國經濟（即本集團
uncertain outcome of the November presidential election,              主要市場）也有持續的隱憂。儘管挑戰重重，如無
indicating a sustained vulnerability and fragility in the American    任何不可預見的情況，本集團將努力不懈地實現又
economy, being the Group’s major market. However, as                  一年的穩定業務增長，並繼續堅定不移地專注於滿
challenging as it can be and barring any unforeseen circumstances,    足客戶需求。
the Group endeavours to achieve yet another year of stable
business growth with a steadfast focus on meeting our customers’
needs.

Although our business is not immune to the effects of inflation and   雖然我們的業務無法免受大部分經濟體所面對由通

The Corporate Governance Code                                            企業管治守則
The Directors confirm that the Company has fully complied with           董事確認本公司已全面遵守上市規則附錄C1所載
the code provisions set out in the Corporate Governance Code (the        之企業管治守則（「守則」）的條文規定，及於適用時
“Code”) attached to the Listing Rules as Appendix C1, and adopted        實行該守則所載的最佳建議常規。年內，主席顧廸
recommended best practices set out in the Code whenever                  安女士由於先前其他業務安排，未能在並無執行董
appropriate. During the year, Ms. Koo Di An, Louise, Chairman,           事出席的情況下與獨立非執行董事召開會議。
was unable to hold a meeting with the Independent Non-Executive
Directors without the presence of the Executive Directors due to
other prior business engagements.

The Board                                                                董事會

for reviewing its effectiveness. Procedures have been designed           訂程序以防止資產未經授權使用或出售、確保存有

provision of reliable financial information for internal use or for      序可合理並非絕對保證不會出現重大誤差、虧損或
publication, and ensuring compliance with applicable laws, rules         欺騙。
and regulations. The procedures provide reasonable but not
absolute assurance against material errors, losses or fraud.

     Risk Management Function                                                 風險管理職能
     The Group has established a robust risk management framework,            本集團已制定穩健的風險管理組織架構，由本集團
     which consists of the Board, the Audit Committee and Senior              董事會、審核委員會及高級管理層組成。董事會釐
     Management of the Group. The Board determines the nature and             定就達成本集團策略目標應承擔的風險性質及程
     extent of risks that shall be taken in achieving the Group’s strategic   度，並全面負責監督風險管理的整體成效。本集團
     objectives, and has the overall responsibility for monitoring the        識別對實現本集團目標造成不利影響的主要風險，
     overall effectiveness of risk management. The Group identifies           並根據一套標準準則評估及排列所識別風險的優先
     key risks that would adversely affect the achievement of the             次序。之後由風險負責人制定風險緩解計劃，以便
     Group’s objectives, and assesses and prioritises the identified          將風險管控至可接受水平。
     risks according to a set of standard criteria. Risk mitigation plans
     are then established by the risk owners to manage the risks to
     acceptable level.

     The Board, through the Audit Committee, has reviewed annually            董事會透過審核委員會對截至二零二三年十二月
     the effectiveness of the Group’s risk management system for the          三十一日止年度本集團風險管理系統的成效進行年
     year ended 31st December 2023. Based on the findings and the             度審閱。根據審核委員會的調查結果及意見，董事
     comments of the Audit Committee, the Board considers that the            會認為本集團的風險管理系統有效及充分。
     Group’s risk management system is effective and adequate.

the transaction of any business specified in such requisition. The     於一位呈請者簽署同一格式之多份文件組成。如董
requisition must state the purposes of the meeting, and must be        事未能於送達請求書二十一日內正式進行召開該大
signed by the requisitionists and deposited at the registered office   會，呈請者或持有超過全部呈請者總投票權半數的
of the Company, and may consist of several documents in like           任何呈請者可以自行召開該大會，惟任何由此召開

     We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Perennial   我們已審計恒都集團有限公司（「貴公司」）及其附屬

     consolidated statement of changes in equity and the consolidated

     consolidated financial statements, including material accounting
     policy information.

     In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true     我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據香港會計師
     and fair view of the financial position of the Group as at 31st       公會（「香港會計師公會」）頒佈的香港財務報告準則

     for the year then ended in accordance with Hong Kong Financial        團於二零二三年十二月三十一日的財務狀況以及其
     Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”) issued by the Hong Kong                截至該日止年度的財務表現及現金流量，並已遵照
     Institute of Certified Public Accountants (the “HKICPA”) and          公司條例的披露規定妥為編製。
     have been properly prepared in compliance with the disclosure

     Basis for Opinion                                                    意見的基礎
     We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards         我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的香港審計準則
     on Auditing (“HKSAs”) issued by the HKICPA. Our responsibilities     （「香港審計準則」）進行審計。我們在該等準則下承
     under those standards are further described in the “Auditor’s         擔的責任已在本報告「核數師就審計綜合財務報表

     Statements” section of our report. We are independent of the          會頒佈的專業會計師道德守則（「守則」），我們獨立
     Group in accordance with the HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for              於 貴集團，並已履行守則中的其他專業道德責
     Professional Accountants (the “Code”), and we have fulfilled our      任。我們相信，我們所獲得的審計憑證能充足及適

Key Audit Matters (Continued)                                           關鍵審計事項（續）

 Key Audit Matter                                              How our audit addressed the Key Audit Matter
 關鍵審計事項                                                        我們的審計如何處理關鍵審計事項

 We identified the impairment of land and buildings as
 a key audit matter because of its significance to the
 consolidated financial statements and the judgement and
 estimation involved in the impairment review and test
 of land and buildings including the consideration of the
 indicators of impairment and the determination of the key
 assumptions applied in the impairment model.
 我們識別土地及樓宇的減值為關鍵審計事項，乃基於其對
 綜合財務報表的重要性以及土地及樓宇的減值檢討及測試
 時所涉及的判斷及估計（包括考慮減值指標及釐定減值模
 型所應用的重大假設）。

 Related disclosures are included in notes 2.8, 4.5, 4.6
 and 15 to the consolidated financial statements.
 有關披露分別載於綜合財務報表附註2.8、4.5、4.6及15。

     1. General Information                                                    1. 一般資料
          Perennial International Limited (the “Company”) is an                  恒都集團有限公司（「本公司」）為投資控股公
          investment holding company. The Company and its                        司。本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）主
          subsidiaries (together the “Group”) are principally engaged            要從事製造及買賣電線及導線產品。其附屬
          in the manufacturing and trading of electric cable and wire            公司之主要業務載於綜合財務報表附註31。
          products. The principal activities of its subsidiaries are set out
          in note 31 to the consolidated financial statements.

          The Company is a limited liability company incorporated                本公司於百慕達註冊成立為一間有限公
          in Bermuda and its shares are listed on the Main Board                 司，其股份於香港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯
          of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock                 交所」）主板上市。其註冊辦事處的地址為
          Exchange”). The address of its registered office is Clarendon          Clarendon House, 2 Church Street, Hamilton
          House, 2 Church Street, Hamilton HM11, Bermuda.                        HM11, Bermuda。

          These consolidated financial statements are presented                  除另有註明外，此等綜合財務報表以港元
          in Hong Kong dollars (“HK$”) and all amounts have been                （「港元」）呈列，而所有金額均約整至最接近
          rounded to the nearest thousand, unless otherwise stated.              千元。

                “HKICPA”), accounting principles generally accepted                   表亦遵守聯交所證券上市規則（「上市規

                Companies Ordinance (the “CO”). These consolidated

        2.2.3 Change in accounting policy for valuation of                   2.2.3 土地及樓宇估值的會計政策變更
              land and buildings
              The Group re-assessed its accounting policy for                     本集團於初始確認後重新評估其

2. Summary of Principal Accounting Policies                        2. 主要會計政策概要（續）
   (Continued)
   2.3 Principles of consolidation                                    2.3 綜合原則
       Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured                 附屬公司指本集團對其具有控制權的所
       entities) over which the Group has control. The Group               有實體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團因
       controls an entity when the Group is exposed to, or                 為參與該實體而承擔可變回報的風險或
       has rights to, variable returns from its involvement with           享有可變回報的權益，並有能力透過
       the entity and has the ability to affect those returns              其對該實體活動的主導權影響此等回報
       through its power to direct the activities of the entity.           時，本集團即控制該實體。附屬公司在
       Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the date on                控制權轉移至本集團當日起全面綜合入
       which control is transferred to the Group. They are                 賬。附屬公司在控制權終止當日起停止
       deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                   綜合入賬。

       Intercompany transactions, balances and unrealised                  集團內公司之間的交易、結餘及交易的
       gains on transactions between group companies are                   未變現收益予以對銷。未變現虧損亦予
       eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated,                  以對銷，除非該交易有證據顯示已轉讓
       unless the transaction provides evidence of an                      資產出現減值。附屬公司的會計政策已
       impairment of the transferred assets. Accounting                    在有需要時變更，以確保與本集團所採
       policies of subsidiaries have been changed where                    納政策貫徹一致。
       necessary to ensure consistency with the policies
       adopted by the Group.

   2.4 Separate financial statements                                  2.4 獨立財務報表
       Investments in subsidiaries are accounted for at cost               於附屬公司的投資按成本減減值列賬。
       less impairment. Cost includes direct attributable costs            成本包括直接應佔投資成本。附屬公司
       of investment. The results of subsidiaries are accounted            的業績由本公司根據已收及應收股息列
       for by the Company on the basis of dividend received                賬。
       and receivable.

       Impairment testing of the investments in subsidiaries               如股息超過宣派股息期內附屬公司的全
       is required upon receiving a dividend from these                    面收益總額，或倘在獨立財務報表的投
       investments if the dividend exceeds the total                       資賬面值超過綜合財務報表中被投資公
       comprehensive income of the subsidiary in the period                司淨資產（包括商譽）的賬面值，則必須
       the dividend is declared or if the carrying amount of               於接收該等投資的股息後對附屬公司投
       the investment in the separate financial statements                 資作減值測試。
       exceeds the carrying amount in the consolidated
       financial statements of the investee’s net assets
       including goodwill.

   2.5 Segment reporting                                              2.5 分部報告
       Operating segments are reported in a manner                         經營分部按照與首席營運決策者提供之
       consistent with the internal reporting provided to the              內部報告一致的方式報告。
       chief operating decision-maker.

          2.6 Foreign currency translation                                     2.6 外幣換算
                (i)    Functional and presentation currency                      (i)     功能及呈報貨幣
                       Items included in the financial statements of each                所有本集團實體各自財務報表中
                       of the Group’s entities are measured using the                    的項目均按有關實體營運所在
                       currency of the primary economic environment                      的主要經濟環境的貨幣（「功能貨
                       in which the entity operates (the “functional                     幣」）計算。綜合財務報表則以港
                       currency”). The consolidated financial statements                 元作呈列，其為本公司的功能及
                       are presented in HK$, which is the Company’s                      本集團的呈報貨幣。
                       functional and the Group’s presentation currency.

                (ii)   Transactions and balances                                 (ii)    交易及結餘
                       Foreign currency transactions are translated into                 外幣交易按交易日期的匯率換算
                       the functional currency using the exchange rates                  為功能貨幣。因此等交易的結算
                       at the dates of the transactions. Foreign exchange                以及因以外幣為本位的貨幣資產
                       gains and losses resulting from the settlement                    及負債按年終匯率進行換算而產
                       of such transactions and from the translation of                  生的外匯盈虧一般於損益中確認。
                       monetary assets and liabilities denominated in
                       foreign currencies at year end exchange rate are
                       generally recognised in profit or loss.

                       All foreign exchange gains and losses are                         所有外匯盈虧於綜合收益表的其
                       presented in the consolidated income statement                    他經營開支淨額內按淨額基準呈
                       on a net basis within other operating expenses,                   列。
                       net.

                       Non-monetary items that are measured at fair                      按公允值計量並以外幣計值的非
                       value in a foreign currency are translated using                  貨幣性項目採用公允值確定當日
                       the exchange rates at the date when the fair                      的匯率換算。按公允值列賬的資
                       value was determined. Translation differences                     產及負債的匯兌差異乃作為公允
                       on assets and liabilities carried at fair value are               值盈虧的一部分列報。
                       reported as part of the fair value gain or loss.

                (iii) Group companies                                            (iii)   集團公司
                      The results and financial position of foreign                      境外業務（當中概無持有嚴重通脹
                      operations (none of which has the currency of a                    經濟體的貨幣）如持有與呈報貨幣
                      hyperinflationary economy) that have a functional                  不一致的功能貨幣，其業績和財
                      currency different from the presentation currency                  務狀況均按以下方法兌換為呈報
                      are translated into the presentation currency as                   貨幣：
                      follows:

                       i)   assets and liabilities for each statement of                 i)   每項財務狀況表之資產及
                            financial position presented are translated at                    負債均按該財務狀況表結
                            the closing rate at the date of that statement                    算日的匯率折算為呈報貨
                            of financial position;                                            幣；

            comprehensive income. When a foreign operation                          償還構成淨投資一部分的任何借

            investment are repaid, the associated exchange                          損益，作為出售損益的一部分。
            differences are reclassified to profit or loss, as
            part of the gain or loss on sale.

   2.10 Impairment of non-financial assets                             2.10 非財務資產之減值
       At the end of each reporting period, the Group reviews               於各報告期末，本集團會審閱內部及外
       internal and external sources of information to assess               部資訊來源，從而評估其物業、廠房及
       whether there is any indication that its property, plant             設備有否出現可能減值或先前已確認的
       and equipment may be impaired or impairment loss                     減值虧損已不再存在或可能減少的任何
       previously recognised no longer exists or may be                     跡象。如存在任何該等跡象，資產的可
       reduced. If any such indication exists, the recoverable              收回金額會根據其公允值減去出售成本
       amount of the asset is estimated, based on the higher                及其使用價值的較高者估計得出。當個
       of its fair value less costs of disposal and value in use.           別資產的可收回金額不可能被估計時，
       Where it is not possible to estimate the recoverable                 本集團會獨立估算產生現金流量之最小
       amount of an individual asset, the Group estimates the               資產組別的可收回金額（即現金產生單
       recoverable amount of the smallest group of assets                   位）
                                                                             。
       that generates cash flows independently (i.e. cash-
       generating unit).

       If the recoverable amount of an asset or a cash-                     如估計資產或現金產生單位的可收回金
       generating unit is estimated to be less than its carrying            額低於其賬面值，該資產或現金產生單
       amount, the carrying amount of the asset or cash-                    位之賬面值調低至其可收回金額。減值
       generating unit is reduced to its recoverable amount.                虧損即時確認為開支，惟按重估金額列
       Impairment losses are recognised as an expense                       值的相關資產除外，於有關情況下，減
       immediately, except where the relevant asset is carried              值虧損根據與資產有關的會計政策視作
       at revalued amount in which case the impairment loss                 重估減少。
       is treated as a revaluation decrease in accordance with
       the accounting policy relevant to that asset.

       A reversal of impairment loss is limited to the carrying             減值虧損的撥回以該項資產或現金產生
       amount of the asset or cash-generating unit that would               單位在過往年度未確認減值虧損而應釐
       have been determined had no impairment loss been                     定的賬面值為限。減值虧損的撥回即時

            risk
            In assessing whether the credit risk on a                                評估金融工具的信貸風險自初步

            instrument as at the reporting date with the risk                        金融工具發生違約的風險比較。
            of a default occurring on the financial instrument                       作出此評估時，本集團會同時考
            as at the date of initial recognition. In making                         慮合理和可靠的定量及定性資
            this assessment, the Group considers both                                料，包括無須付出過多成本或努
            quantitative and qualitative information that is                         力後即可獲得的歷史經驗及前瞻

            experience and forward-looking information                               資料：
            that is available without undue cost or effort. In

                  environment that have or may have a                                    變化而會或可能會對債務

                     Credit-impaired financial asset                                      已信貸減值的財務資產
                     A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or                     當發生對財務資產預計未來現金
                     more events that have a detrimental impact on                        流量有不利影響的一個或多個事
                     the estimated future cash flows of that financial                    件時，財務資產出現信貸減值。
                     asset have occurred. Evidence that a financial                       財務資產出現信貸減值的證據包
                     asset is credit-impaired include observable data                     括有關下列事件的可觀察資料：
                     about the following events:

                     (a)   significant financial difficulty of the issuer or              (a)   發行人或借方出現嚴重財
                           the borrower.                                                        政困難。

                     (b)   a breach of contract, such as a default or                     (b)   違反合約，如拖欠或逾期
                           past due event.                                                      事件。

                     (c)   the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic                    (c)   借款人的貸款人因有關借
                           or contractual reasons relating to the                               款人財務困難的經濟或合
                           borrower’s financial difficulty, having                              約理由，而授予借款人原
                           granted to the borrower a concession(s) that                         先貸款人不會另行考慮的
                           the lender(s) would not otherwise consider.                          優惠。

                     (d)   it is becoming probable that the borrower                      (d)   借款人可能陷入破產或其
                           will enter bankruptcy or other financial                             他財務重組。
                           reorganisation.

                     (e)   the disappearance of an active market for                      (e)   因為財政困難而導致財務
                           that financial asset because of financial                            資產失去活躍市場。
                           difficulties.

                     (f)   the purchase or origination of a financial                     (f)   以大幅折讓購買或源生一

                           incurred credit losses.                                              已發生的信貸虧損。

          2.12 Offsetting financial instruments                                  2.12 抵銷金融工具
                Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net            當本集團現時有法定可執行權力可抵銷
                amount is reported in the consolidated statement                   已確認金額，並有意圖按淨額基準結算
                of financial position where the Group currently has                或同時變現資產和結算負債時，財務
                a legally enforceable right to offset the recognised               資產與負債可互相抵銷，並在綜合財務
                amounts, and there is an intention to settle on a                  狀況表呈報其淨額。本集團亦訂立不符
                net basis or realise the asset and settle the liability            合抵銷標準的安排，但在部分情況下仍
                simultaneously. The Group has also entered into                    允許相關金額抵銷，例如破產或終止合
                arrangements that do not meet the criteria for offsetting          約。
                but still allow for the related amounts to be set off
                in certain circumstances, such as bankruptcy or the
                termination of a contract.

   2.19 Borrowings                                                     2.19 借款
       Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value,                   借款最初以淨交易成本的公允值而確
       net of transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are                    認。借款隨後被定為分攤成本；在進款
       subsequently stated at amortised cost; any difference               （扣除交易成本）與贖回價值間之任何差
       between the proceeds (net of transaction costs) and                  異於借貸期的綜合收益表內採用實際利
       the redemption value is recognised in the consolidated               率法而確認。
       income statement over the period of the borrowings
       using the effective interest method.

       Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are                倘貸款融資很有可能部分或全部被提
       recognised as transaction costs of the loan to the                   取，則就設立貸款融資時支付的費用確
       extent that it is probable that some or all of the facility          認為貸款交易成本。費用遞延至提取發
       will be drawn down. The fee is deferred until the draw               生為止。倘無任何證據顯示該融資很有
       down occurs. To the extent there is no evidence that                 可能部分或全部提取，則該費用撥充資
       it is probable that some or all of the facility will be              本作為流動資金服務的預付款項，並於
       drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a prepayment for               有關融資期間攤銷。
       liquidity services and amortised over the period of the
       facility to which it relates.

       Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the          除非本集團可無條件將負債的結算遞延
       Group has an unconditional right to defer settlement                 至報告期末後最少12個月，否則借款
       of the liability for at least twelve months after the                分類為流動負債。
       reporting period.

       General and specific borrowing costs that are directly               直接歸因於收購、興建或生產合資格資
       attributable to the acquisition, construction or                     產的一般及特定借款成本，於要求完成

       the period of time that is required to complete and                  資本化。合資格資產指必須經相當長一
       prepare the asset for its intended use or sale. Qualifying           段時間準備方可達致其擬定用途或銷售
       assets are assets that necessarily take a substantial                之資產。
       period of time to get ready for their intended use or

       period in which they are incurred.                                   銷。

   2.21 Taxation                                                       2.21 稅項
       The income tax expense or credit for the period is the               期內所得稅開支或抵免指根據各司法權
       tax payable on the current period’s taxable income                   區的適用所得稅率按即期應課稅收入支
       based on the applicable income tax rate for each                     付的稅項，而有關所得稅率經暫時差異
       jurisdiction adjusted by changes in deferred tax assets              及未動用稅務虧損所致的遞延稅項資產
       and liabilities attributable to temporary differences and            及負債變動而調整。
       to unused tax losses.

       (a)   Current income tax                                             (a)   當期稅項
             The current income tax charge is calculated on                       當期所得稅支出根據本公司及其
             the basis of the tax laws enacted or substantively                   附屬公司營運及產生應課稅收入

             in the countries where the Company and its                           頒佈的稅務法例計算。管理層就
             subsidiaries operate and generate taxable income.                    適用稅務法例詮釋所規限的情況
             Management periodically evaluates positions                          定期評估報稅表的狀況，並考慮
             taken in tax returns with respect to situations                      稅務機關是否將接納不確定稅項
             in which applicable tax regulation is subject                        處理。本集團根據最可能金額或
             to interpretation and considers whether it is                        預期數值計量稅項結餘，所採用
             probable that a taxation authority will accept an                    方法則取決於可就解決不確定因
             uncertain tax treatment. The Group measures                          素提供較佳預測者。
             its tax balances either based on the most likely
             amount or the expected value, depending on
             which method provides a better prediction of the
             resolution of the uncertainty.

       (b)   Deferred income tax                                            (b)   遞延稅項
             Deferred income tax is provided in full, using the                   遞延所得稅利用負債法就資產和
             liability method, on temporary differences arising                   負債的稅基與在資產和負債在綜
             between the tax bases of assets and liabilities                      合財務報表的賬面值所產生的暫
             and their carrying amounts in the consolidated                       時差異作出全額撥備。然而，遞
             financial statements. However, deferred income                       延所得稅來自在交易（不包括企業
             tax is not accounted for if it arises from initial                   合併）中對資產或負債的初步確
             recognition of an asset or liability in a transaction                認，而在交易時不影響會計損益
             other than a business combination that at the                        或應課稅盈虧，且不會產生相等
             time of the transaction affects neither accounting                   應課稅及可扣稅暫時差額，則不
             nor taxable profit or loss and does not give                         作記賬。遞延稅項採用在報告期
             rise to equal taxable and deductible temporary                       末前已頒佈或實質頒佈，並在有
             differences. Deferred income tax is determined                       關之遞延所得稅資產實現或遞延
             using tax rates (and laws) that have been enacted                    所得稅負債結算時預期將會適用
             or substantively enacted by the end of reporting                     之稅率（及法例）
                                                                                         而釐定。
             period and are expected to apply when the related
             deferred income tax asset is realised or the

             The deferred tax liability in relation to investment                 有關按公允值計量的投資物業的

          2.22 Provision                                                          2.22 撥備
                Provisions are recognised when the Group has a                       當本集團因已發生的事件須承擔現有之
                present legal or constructive obligation as a result of              法律性或推定性的責任，而解除責任時
                past events, it is probable that an outflow of resources             有可能消耗資源，並在責任金額能夠可
                will be required to settle the obligation, and a reliable            靠地作出估算的情況下，需確立撥備。
                estimate of the amount can be made. Provisions are not               但不會就未來經營虧損確認撥備。
                recognised for future operating losses.

                Where there are a number of similar obligations, the                 如有多項類似債務，其需要在結算中有
                likelihood that an outflow will be required in settlement            資源流出的可能性，則可根據債務的類
                is determined by considering the class of obligations as             別整體考慮。即使在同一債務類別所包
                a whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood            含的任何一個項目相關的資源流出的可
                of an outflow with respect to any one item included in               能性極低，仍須確認撥備。
                the same class of obligations may be small.

                Provisions are measured at the present value of the                  撥備按管理層對於報告期末須償付現有
                management’s best estimate of the expenditures                       責任的支出的最佳估計的現值計量。用
                required to settle the obligation present at the end                 於釐定現值的貼現率為反映當時市場對
                of the reporting period. The discount rate used to                   金錢時間值及負債特定風險的評估的稅
                determine the present value is a pre-tax rate that                   前利率。隨時間流逝而增加的撥備確認
                reflects current market assessments of the time value                為利息開支。
                of money and the risks specific to the liability. The
                increase in the provision due to passage of time is
                recognised as interest expense.

                The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial       本集團因經營業務而承受不同財務風
                risks: market risk (including foreign exchange risk and          險，即市場風險（包括外匯風險及價格

                unpredictability of financial markets and seeks to               的不可預知性，務求盡量減低對本集團

                financial performance.

                the Group.

3. Financial Risk Management (Continued)                             3. 財務風險管理（續）
   3.2 Capital risk management                                         3.2 資金風險管理
      The primary objective of the Group’s capital                          本集團資金管理的主要目標為確保本集
      management is to safeguard the Group’s ability to                     團持續營運，維持良好的信貸評級和穩
      continue as a going concern, maintain a strong credit                 健的資金比率，以支持其業務及提升股
      rating and a healthy capital ratio to support the business            東價值。
      and to enhance shareholder value.

      The Group manages its capital structure and makes                     本集團按經濟狀況及業務策略之變動管
      adjustments to it in light of changes in economic                     理其資本架構，並對其作出調整。為維
      conditions and business strategies. To maintain or                    持或調整資本架構，本集團可能調整向
      adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust the                股東派付之股息、向股東返還資本、發
      dividend payments to shareholders, return capital to                  行新股份或出售資產以減少債務。本集
